# Chizl.EmojiLive
This is a library of structs with over 4000 Emoji with it.  These structs and all the xml documentation is auto generated by another internal app by Chizl.  If the dates/times change, it might not reflect that there was a modification to the struct, but could of just been regenerated based on the internal app.

**TargetFrameworks**: netstandard2.0;netstandard2.1;net471;net472;net48;net481;net8.0;net9.0

This is quick access to the following structs, with sub-groups, and a total of 4098 Emoji:
- Basic Latin, (29) Subgroups, (317) fully-qualified Emoji
- Smileys & Emotion, (16) Subgroups, (169) fully-qualified Emoji
- People & Body, (16) Subgroups, (2261) fully-qualified Emoji
- Animals & Nature, (8) Subgroups, (159) fully-qualified Emoji
- Food & Drink, (7) Subgroups, (131) fully-qualified Emoji
- Travel & Places, (11) Subgroups, (218) fully-qualified Emoji
- Activities, (5) Subgroups, (85) fully-qualified Emoji
- Objects, (18) Subgroups, (264) fully-qualified Emoji
- Symbols, (14) Subgroups, (224) fully-qualified Emoji
- Flags, (3) Subgroups, (270) fully-qualified Emoji

Each Emoji has detailed properties.  This library works with Console.Write() or Form Display with use of EmojiPngImage.<br/>
The library can return to you a 64x64 full color png image of the Unicode character.

**IMPORTANT NOTICE**: Even though this library can return PNG images of the Emoji through EmojiPngImage property, not all images come back with <u>TONE</u>.  Also found, Emoji like PeopleBody.FamilyWomanWomanGirlGirl only show one woman.<br/>
Using only the EmojiCharacter property in console, will show <u>TONE</u> and both Form Labels using Emoji font's will see the complex Emoji.  Label's can't show the color, but will display the character in the foreground color selected for the label.  It's not apparent at this time why images are missing this feature.  It is something that I'm aware of and it's being researched.

## List of Methods:
- SaveEmoji - `Uses SkiaSharp, a cross platform library.  This will save the current emoji to the specified file path.  If fileName is left null, Emoji.Name will be used instead.`
    - param name="fullPath": Path only without filename.  (e.g. c:\\myimages, .\\myimages)
    - param name="fileName: Filename: if null name will default to '{{Emoji.Name}}.{{imageFormat}}'.
    - param name="imageFormat": Multiple formats to choose from.  File extension will be replaced with format type.
    - param name="overWrite": (Optional) Overwrite existing file. Default: true - will Overwrite existing file if exists.


## List of Properties:
- string Group - `Emoji group name, set by unicode.org`
- string SubGroup - `Emoji subgroup name, set by unicode.org`
- string Name - `Emoji name, cleaned up, based on unicode.org. This is also used as the resource name.`
- string FullName - `Emoji full name, set by unicode.org`
- string Version - `Emoji version, set by unicode.org`
- string CodePoints - `All Emoji code points, set by unicode.org`
- string UnqualifiedCodePoints - `Shortest Unqualifed Emoji code points, set by unicode.org`
- byte[] EmojiPngImage - `Emoji 64x64 Image byte array in PNG format.`
- int EmojiDisplayWidth - `Display width is the actual width on screen of this Emoji.  Where length could be 12, display width might be only 1.`
- string EmojiCharacter - `Emoji unicode character as combined code points`
- string UnqualifiedEmojiCharacter - `Shortest unqualified Emoji unicode character as combined code points`
- string ErrorMessage - `If HasError is true, then the errormessage will show here.`
- int[] UTF32Codes - `Qualified Emoji array of decimal values for each unicode byte for the EmojiCharacter.`
- int Length - `Emoji string length`
- ByteFlag[] ByteFlags - `Each ByteFlag represents 1 byte either single char or multi-byte Char if exists.`
- bool FullyQualified - `Emoji status, set by unicode.org.`
- bool HasError - `By passing one or multiple flag into HasFlag, a full scan or the emoji will return true if 'all' flags passed are found.`
- bool HasUnqualifiedCharacter - `True, if any byte failed to convert. Enumerating through ByteStatus, this will state which byte failed.`
- bool UsesZWJ - `Indicates whether the emoji includes a Zero Width Joiner (ZWJ) character (U+200D), which is used to form composite emojis (e.g., family, professions, or gender variants).`
- bool UsesVariationSelector - `Indicates whether the emoji uses Variation Selector-16 (U+FE0F), which requests an emoji-style (color) rendering instead of plain text.`
- bool UsesKeycapCombiner - `Indicates whether the emoji uses the Keycap Combining character (U+20E3), typically used with digits or symbols to form keycap-style emojis (e.g., 1️⃣, *️⃣).`
- bool IsSingleCodepoint - `Indicates whether the emoji consists of only a single Unicode code point, as opposed to a sequence (e.g., ZWJ-based or keycap emojis).`
- bool CanRendersAsImage - `Returns true if the emoji appears to render as an image (glyph) on the current platform, based on a visual comparison between its output and a fallback glyph.`


## /Demos/ - Subfolder
- Windows Form Framework 4.81 Demo - `.\demos\Framework48FormsDemo\`
- Console NET8.0 Demo - `.\demos\ConsoleDemo\`

## Written in
- Visual Studio 2022 Professional on Win11

## Supports
- netstandard2.0
- netstandard2.1
- net47
- net48
- net8.0
- net9.0
 
## Dependencies
- Nuget: SkiaSharp

## Example of use
 ```csharp
using Chizl.EmojiLive;

    Emoji emoji = EmojiActivities.JackOLantern;
    Console.WriteLine($"[{emoji}] Usage: {emoji.Group}.{emoji.Name} \n" +
                            $"Name: {emoji.Name}, Display Name: \"{emoji.FullName}\"\n" +
                            $"String Length: {emoji.Length}, Display Width: {emoji.EmojiDisplayWidth}, Version: {emoji.Version}\n" +
                            $"Group: {emoji.Group}, Subgroup: {emoji.SubGroup}\n" +
                            $"CodePoints: {emoji.CodePoints}, Codes: {string.Join(", ", emoji.UTF32Codes)}\n" +
                            $"Flags: ({String.Join(", ", emoji.ByteFlags)})\n");

// Response:
// [🎃] Usage: Activities.JackOLantern
// Name: JackOLantern, Display Name: "Jack-O-Lantern"
// String Length: 2, Display Width: 2, Version: E0.6
// Group: Activities, Subgroup: Event
// CodePoints: 1F383, Codes: 127875
// Flags: (HighSurrogate, LowSurrogate, Surrogate, SurrogatePair, Symbol)
```
